> function valid_graph(num, edgeset, roots); // input is number of vertices, set of ordered pairs of directed edges
function>                                       // and distinguished vertices called roots
function>     valid := true;
function>     for i := 1 to num do
function|for>   if #[j: j in edgeset | j[1] eq i] gt 1 or #[j: j in edgeset | j[2] eq i] gt 1 then
function|for|if>                // A valid graph can have no more than one directed edge pointing to a given vertex or from a given vertex
function|for|if>            valid := false;
function|for|if>            break;
function|for|if>        end if;
function|for>   if #[j: j in edgeset | j[1] eq i] eq 0 and i in roots eq false then
function|for|if>                // Any vertex of a valid graph with no edge pointing out of it must be a root
function|for|if>            valid := false;
function|for|if>            break;
function|for|if>        end if;
function|for>     end for;
function>     if valid eq true then
function|if>    return true;
function|if>     else
function|if>    return false;
function|if>     end if;
function> end function;
> 
> function matrix_potentials(wphs);
function>     deg := wphs[1];
function>     num_weights := #wphs - 1; // This gives the number of weights (dimension of hypersurf + 2)
function>     alledges := {}; // This is a set where we'll store edges in the graph corresponding to f
function>     roots := [];
function>     for i := 1 to num_weights do
function|for>   weight := wphs[i+1];
function|for>   if deg mod weight eq 0 then
function|for|if>            Append(~roots,i); // The weights which divide the degree are the possible "roots" of valid graphs
function|for|if>        end if;
function|for>   set := [1 .. num_weights];
function|for>   Exclude(~set,i);
function|for>   for j in set do // This searches other weights so that d-a_j is divisible by weight
function|for|for>           if (deg - wphs[j+1]) mod weight eq 0 then
function|for|for|if>            Include(~alledges, [i,j]); // A valid edge is i -> j where X_i^a X_j is a monomial of degree d
function|for|for|if>        end if;
function|for|for>       end for;
function|for>     end for;
function>     matrix_list := [];
function>     P := {{}};
function>     for i := 1 to num_weights do //At most n edges are present in a valid graph (one pointing out of each vertex)
function|for>   P := P join Subsets(alledges,i);
function|for>     end for;
function>     for edgeset in P do //We'll test possible edge sets for valid potentials
function|for>   if valid_graph(num_weights,edgeset, roots) eq true then
function|for|if>            matrix := [[]: i in [1 .. num_weights]];
function|for|if>            usedroots := [i: i in [1 .. num_weights] | #[j: j in edgeset | j[1] eq i] eq 0];
function|for|if>            for i in usedroots do
function|for|if|for>            monomial := [0: j in [1 .. num_weights]];
function|for|if|for>            monomial[i] := deg / wphs[i+1];
function|for|if|for>            matrix[i] := monomial;
function|for|if|for>        end for;
function|for|if>            for edge in edgeset do
function|for|if|for>            monomial := [0: j in [1 .. num_weights]];
function|for|if|for>            monomial[edge[1]] := (deg - wphs[edge[2]+1]) / wphs[edge[1] +1];
function|for|if|for>            monomial[edge[2]] := 1;
function|for|if|for>            matrix[edge[1]] := monomial;
function|for|if|for>        end for;
function|for|if>            Append(~matrix_list, matrix);
function|for|if>        end if;
function|for>     end for;
function>     return [Matrix(RationalField(), num_weights, num_weights, potential): potential in matrix_list];
function> end function;
> 
> function WphsPolytope(weights,deg)
function> dim := #weights - 2;
function> exp := deg div weights[1]; 
function> if deg mod weights[1] eq 0 then
function|if> tordiv := [exp] cat [0: i in [1 .. dim+1]];
function|if> else
function|if> remainder := [j:j in [2 .. dim+2] | (deg - weights[j]) mod weights[1] eq 0];
function|if> other := remainder[1];
function|if> tordiv := [(deg-weights[other])/weights[1]] cat [0: i in [1 .. dim+1]];
function|if> tordiv[other] := 1;
function|if> end if;
function> PP := WeightedProjectiveSpace(Rationals(),weights);
function> OD := Divisor(PP,tordiv); //Weil divisor which is section of O(D)
function> Q := RiemannRochPolytope(OD);
function> return Q;
function> end function;
> 
> function EquationToPolytope(weights,A) //This function takes in an equation of a wphs and
function>                                       //outputs the Newton polytope
function> PP := WeightedProjectiveSpace(Rationals(),weights);
function> raymatrix := Matrix(Rays(PP));
function> vertices := [ElementToSequence(Solution(Transpose(raymatrix),Vector(Integers(),[A[i][j]-A[1][j]:j in [1 .. 5]]))):i in [1 .. 5]];
function> return Polytope(vertices);
function> end function;

> //Repeat of Run 1, but recording the new irrational polytopes we get along the way 
> //(requires another function: EquationToPolytope)
> //Run 1: Testing for IJ obstruction using automorphisms of prime order
> //Assumption: we have h^{1,2} = g, prime = 2g + 1 is prime, Aut(X) contains an element of order prime = 2g+1,
> //and the hypersurface X has singularities only at isolated points or along genus zero curves
> //Required functions: matrix_potentials, EquationToPolytope, WphsPolytope
> //Note: the actions computed here are on the duals of the tangent spaces to intermediate Jacobians and Jacobians,
> //respectively.
> status_list := [];
> irrational_polytope_list := [];
> for i in [1 .. 4442] do //insert here which families to test
for> print i;
for> status := "Unknown";
for> weights := sporadic[i];
for> deg := &+weights - 1;
for> //Part 0: Check singularities of family
for> //X is well-formed, hence smooth outside of codim 2, so the only strata to check are those
for> //where X intersects a 2D stratum with corresponding weights having a gcd > 1
for> maxgenus := 0;
for> for a in [1 .. 5] do
for|for> for b in [a+1 .. 5] do
for|for|for> sub := Remove(Remove(weights, b),a);
for|for|for> if Gcd(sub) gt 1 then
for|for|for|if> wred := [w div Gcd(sub):w in sub];
for|for|for|if> dred := deg div Gcd(sub);
for|for|for|if> while Max([Gcd(wred[1],wred[2]),Gcd(wred[1],wred[3]),Gcd(wred[2],wred[3])]) gt 1 do
for|for|for|if|while> offenders := [i: i in [[1,2],[1,3],[2,3]] | Gcd(wred[i[1]],wred[i[2]]) gt 1];
for|for|for|if|while> o := offenders[1];
for|for|for|if|while> g := Gcd(wred[o[1]],wred[o[2]]);
for|for|for|if|while> wred[o[1]] := wred[o[1]] div g;
for|for|for|if|while> wred[o[2]] := wred[o[2]] div g;
for|for|for|if|while> dred := dred div g;
for|for|for|if|while> end while;
for|for|for|if> P := WphsPolytope(wred,dred);
for|for|for|if> maxgenus := Max(maxgenus,#InteriorPoints(P));
for|for|for|if> end if;
for|for|for> end for;
for|for> end for;
for> 
for> if maxgenus eq 0 then
for|if> 
for|if> for A in matrix_potentials([deg] cat weights) do
for|if|for> 
for|if|for> //Part 1: Find monomial basis for h^{1,2}(X)
for|if|for> k := Nrows(A);
for|if|for> p := 1; //value for the Hodge h^{q,p} we want to calculate
for|if|for> P<[x]> := PolynomialRing(Rationals(),weights);
for|if|for> f := &+[&*[x[j]^(Floor(A[i][j])): j in [1 .. k]]: i in [1 .. k]];
for|if|for> mons := MonomialsOfWeightedDegree(P,(p+1)*deg - &+weights);
for|if|for> V := VectorSpace(Rationals(),#mons); //Vector space of polynomials of given degree
for|if|for> spanlist := [];
for|if|for> for j in [1 .. k] do
for|if|for|for> deriv := Derivative(f,x[j]);
for|if|for|for> multipliers := MonomialsOfWeightedDegree(P,(p+1)*deg-&+weights-(deg-weights[j]));
for|if|for|for> for term in multipliers do
for|if|for|for|for> Append(~spanlist,[MonomialCoefficient(term*deriv,mon): mon in mons]);
for|if|for|for|for> end for;
for|if|for|for> end for;
for|if|for> W := sub<V | spanlist>;
for|if|for> quotient, psi := V/W;
for|if|for> basis_indices := [];
for|if|for> for aa in [1 .. #mons] do //Construct a monomial basis of H^{q,p}(X) by discarding dependent monomials
for|if|for|for> if IsIndependent([psi(V.l): l in basis_indices cat [aa]]) eq true then
for|if|for|for|if> Append(~basis_indices, aa);
for|if|for|for|if> end if;
for|if|for|for> end for;
for|if|for> basis := mons[basis_indices];
for|if|for> h12 := #basis;
for|if|for> 
for|if|for> //Part 2: Find automorphism group of X
for|if|for> v := Matrix(Rationals(),k,1,[1: i in [1 .. k]]); //all ones vector
for|if|for> q := A^(-1)*v;
for|if|for> charges := ElementToSequence(q); //Charges vector for ambient weighted projective space
for|if|for> deg := Lcm([Denominator(charge): charge in charges]); //Degree of hypersurface in wps
for|if|for> d := Lcm([Denominator(b): b in ElementToSequence(A^(-1))]); //Degree of Fermat mapping to wphs
for|if|for> weights := [Floor(deg*charge): charge in charges];
for|if|for> B := d*A^(-1);
for|if|for> L := StandardLattice(k);
for|if|for> qvec := L ! [Floor(d*charge): charge in charges];
for|if|for> M := sub<L | [d*L.i: i in [1 .. k]] cat [qvec]>;
for|if|for> N := sub<L | [L ! Transpose(B)[i]: i in [1 .. k]]>;
for|if|for> Aut,phi := N/M; //The automorphism group of the Delsarte hypersurface
for|if|for> order := Order(Aut);
for|if|for> prime := 2*h12+1;
for|if|for> 
for|if|for> if order mod prime eq 0 and prime ge 3 and IsPrime(prime) eq true then
for|if|for|if> 
for|if|for|if> //Part 3: Find eigenvalues of action of order p element on H^{2,1} = T_0(IJ(X))
for|if|for|if> g := [h: h in Aut | Order(h) eq prime][1]; //generator of Z/p subgroup
for|if|for|if> reps := [a*qvec/(d div deg) + g @@ phi: a in [0 .. d-1] | d/Gcd(ElementToSequence(a*qvec/(d div deg) + g @@ phi)) eq Order(g)];
for|if|for|if> gaction := [(x div (d div prime)) mod prime: x in ElementToSequence(reps[1])];
for|if|for|if> twist := &+gaction; //We have to twist by the determinantal action
for|if|for|if> eqvalue := Floor(-(p+1)*(&+[A[1][j]*gaction[j]: j in [1 .. k]])); //twist down by f^{p+1}
for|if|for|if> eigenvalues := {* (&+[gaction[i]*Exponents(basis[j])[i]: i in [1 .. k]] + twist + eqvalue) mod prime : j in [1 .. #basis] *};
for|if|for|if> 
for|if|for|if> 
for|if|for|if> //Part 4: Test to see if eigenvalues match those of the Jacobian of some curve
for|if|for|if> //Source: ON THE CURVES OF GENUS g WITH AUTOMORPHISMS OF PRIME ORDER 2g+1 by Seyama
for|if|for|if> //We compute the eigenvalues on the curve y^r(y-1) = x^prime for each r in 1,...,prime-2
for|if|for|if> match := false;
for|if|for|if> for r in [1 .. prime-2] do
for|if|for|if|for> E := {* e : e in [1 .. prime-1] | (e + (r*e) mod prime) le prime-1 *};
for|if|for|if|for> for s in [1 .. prime - 1] do
for|if|for|if|for|for> Es := {* s*e mod prime: e in E *};
for|if|for|if|for|for> if eigenvalues eq Es then
for|if|for|if|for|for|if> match := true;
for|if|for|if|for|for|if> print weights, deg, A, h12, Aut, gaction, eigenvalues, r, s, "Possibly Rational";
for|if|for|if|for|for|if> status := "Possibly Rational";
for|if|for|if|for|for|if> break r;
for|if|for|if|for|for|if> end if;
for|if|for|if|for|for> end for;
for|if|for|if|for> end for;
for|if|for|if> if match eq false then
for|if|for|if|if> print weights, deg, A, h12, Aut, gaction, eigenvalues, "Irrational";
for|if|for|if|if> status := "Irrational";
for|if|for|if|if> Append(~irrational_polytope_list,<i,weights,deg,A,h12,Vertices(EquationToPolytope(weights,A))>);
for|if|for|if|if> break A;
for|if|for|if|if> end if;
for|if|for|if> 
for|if|for|if> end if;
for|if|for> 
for|if|for> 
for|if|for> end for;
for|if> Append(~status_list,<i,status>);
for|if> end if;
for> 
for> end for;
